@echo off
echo ========================================
echo   Laptop Proxy - Installer Builder
echo ========================================

echo.
echo [1/3] Checking for Inno Setup compiler...
where iscc >nul 2>nul
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo [ERROR] Inno Setup compiler iscc.exe not found in PATH.
    echo Please install Inno Setup from https://jrsoftware.org/isdl.php
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
echo       Found.

echo.
echo [2/3] Checking for portable build output...
if not exist "dist\Hotspot_Bypass_VPN_Windows\Hotspot_Bypass_VPN_Windows.exe" (
    echo [ERROR] dist\Hotspot_Bypass_VPN_Windows\Hotspot_Bypass_VPN_Windows.exe not found.
    echo Please run build_exe.bat first to produce the portable build.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)
echo       Found.

echo.
echo [3/3] Compiling Installer...
iscc laptop_proxy.iss

echo.
echo ========================================
echo   BUILD COMPLETE!
echo   Your installer is in the "Output" folder.
echo ========================================
pause


============================================================
FILE: laptop_proxy\diagnostic.py
============================================================

"""
Hotspot Bypass VPN — Connection Diagnostic Tool

Standalone script that monitors TCP, UDP, and DNS health
through the SOCKS5 proxy while the main app is running.

Usage:
    python diagnostic.py --proxy 192.168.49.1:8080
    python diagnostic.py --proxy 192.168.49.1:8080 --interval 5
"""

import argparse
import socket
import struct
import sys
import time
import threading
import os
from datetime import datetime

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
LOG_DIR = HERE

# Color codes for Windows terminal
class Color:
    GREEN = '\033[92m'
    RED = '\033[91m'
    YELLOW = '\033[93m'
    CYAN = '\033[96m'
    GRAY = '\033[90m'
    BOLD = '\033[1m'
    RESET = '\033[0m'

def supports_color():
    return hasattr(sys.stdout, 'isatty') and sys.stdout.isatty()

C = Color if supports_color() else type('', (), {
    k: '' for k in ['GREEN','RED','YELLOW','CYAN','GRAY','BOLD','RESET']
})()

class Diagnostic:
    def __init__(self, proxy_host, proxy_port, interval=10):
        self.proxy_host = proxy_host
        self.proxy_port = proxy_port
        self.interval = max(1, interval)
        self._running = True
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._results = []
        self.log_file = None

    def _socks5_tcp_connect(self, target_host, target_port, timeout=5):
        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
        s.settimeout(timeout)
        try:
            s.connect((self.proxy_host, self.proxy_port))
            # Handshake
            s.sendall(b'\x05\x01\x00')
            resp = s.recv(2)
            if resp != b'\x05\x00':
                return None, "SOCKS5 auth failed"

            # Connect command
            try:
                ip = socket.inet_aton(target_host)
                cmd = b'\x05\x01\x00\x01' + ip + struct.pack('>H', target_port)
            except OSError:
                host_bytes = target_host.encode()
                cmd = b'\x05\x01\x00\x03' + bytes([len(host_bytes)]) + host_bytes + struct.pack('>H', target_port)

            s.sendall(cmd)
            resp = s.recv(10)
            if len(resp) < 2 or resp[1] != 0x00:
                return None, f"SOCKS5 connect failed (rep={resp[1] if len(resp) > 1 else '?'})"
            return s, None
        except socket.timeout:
            return None, "timeout"
        except ConnectionRefusedError:
            return None, "connection refused"
        except OSError as e:
            return None, str(e)
        except Exception as e:
            return None, str(e)

    def _socks5_udp_test(self, timeout=5):
        """
        Establishes SOCKS5 UDP ASSOCIATE, sends a DNS query to 8.8.8.8:53,
        and waits for a response.
        """
        ctrl = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
        ctrl.settimeout(timeout)
        try:
            ctrl.connect((self.proxy_host, self.proxy_port))
            ctrl.sendall(b'\x05\x01\x00')
            if ctrl.recv(2) != b'\x05\x00':
                ctrl.close()
                return None, "UDP: auth failed"

            # UDP ASSOCIATE command
            ctrl.sendall(b'\x05\x03\x00\x01' + socket.inet_aton('0.0.0.0') + struct.pack('>H', 0))
            resp = ctrl.recv(10)
            if len(resp) < 10 or resp[1] != 0x00:
                ctrl.close()
                return None, f"UDP ASSOCIATE failed (rep={resp[1] if len(resp) > 1 else '?'})"

            relay_port = (resp[8] << 8) | resp[9]

            # Create a UDP socket for relay traffic
            udp_sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
            udp_sock.settimeout(timeout)
            udp_sock.bind(('0.0.0.0', 0))
            _, local_port = udp_sock.getsockname()

            # Build a DNS query packet for google.com (standard DNS query)
            tid = int(time.time() * 1000) & 0xFFFF
            dns_query = struct.pack('>H', tid)  # Transaction ID
            dns_query += struct.pack('>H', 0x0100)  # Flags: standard query, recursion desired
            dns_query += struct.pack('>HHHH', 1, 0, 0, 0)  # Questions=1
            # google.com as DNS question
            for part in b'google.com'.split(b'.'):
                dns_query += bytes([len(part)]) + part
            dns_query += b'\x00'
            dns_query += struct.pack('>HH', 1, 1)  # Type A, Class IN

            # Build SOCKS5 UDP header (RSV+FRAG+ATYP+DST.ADDR+DST.PORT)
            udp_header = b'\x00\x00\x00'  # RSV + FRAG
            udp_header += b'\x01'  # ATYP = IPv4
            udp_header += socket.inet_aton('8.8.8.8')
            udp_header += struct.pack('>H', 53)

            # Send the DNS query through the relay
            udp_sock.sendto(udp_header + dns_query, (self.proxy_host, relay_port))

            # Wait for response
            data, addr = udp_sock.recvfrom(4096)
            # Skip SOCKS5 UDP header (4 bytes RSV+FRAG+ATYP + addr + port)
            if len(data) > 10:
                ctrl.close()
                udp_sock.close()
                return len(data), None

            ctrl.close()
            udp_sock.close()
            return None, "UDP: no valid response data"

        except socket.timeout:
            return None, "UDP: timeout"
        except Exception as e:
            return None, f"UDP: {e}"
        finally:
            try:
                ctrl.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                udp_sock.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _dns_test(self):
        """Resolves roblox.com through the SOCKS5 proxy via TCP CONNECT to 8.8.8.8:53 (DNS-over-TCP)."""
        t0 = time.time()
        conn, err = self._socks5_tcp_connect('8.8.8.8', 53, timeout=5)
        if err:
            return None, err

        try:
            # Build DNS query for roblox.com
            tid = int(time.time() * 1000) & 0xFFFF
            dns_query = struct.pack('>H', tid) + struct.pack('>H', 0x0100)
            dns_query += struct.pack('>HHHH', 1, 0, 0, 0)
            for part in b'roblox.com'.split(b'.'):
                dns_query += bytes([len(part)]) + part
            dns_query += b'\x00'
            dns_query += struct.pack('>HH', 1, 1)

            # DNS-over-TCP requires 2-byte length prefix
            dns_packet = struct.pack('>H', len(dns_query)) + dns_query
            conn.sendall(dns_packet)

            # Read response
            len_bytes = conn.recv(2)
            if len(len_bytes) < 2:
                return None, "DNS: no response length"
            resp_len = (len_bytes[0] << 8) | len_bytes[1]
            resp_data = b''
            while len(resp_data) < resp_len:
                chunk = conn.recv(resp_len - len(resp_data))
                if not chunk:
                    break
                resp_data += chunk

            elapsed = time.time() - t0

            # Parse response for IPs
            if len(resp_data) < 16:
                return None, "DNS: response too short"

            # Parse answer section
            answers = []
            offset = 12  # Skip header
            # Skip question section
            while offset < len(resp_data):
                if resp_data[offset] == 0:
                    offset += 1
                    break
                elif resp_data[offset] & 0xC0:
                    offset += 2
                    break
                else:
                    offset += 1 + resp_data[offset] + 1
            offset += 4  # Skip QTYPE and QCLASS

            # Parse answers
            while offset + 12 <= len(resp_data):
                if resp_data[offset] & 0xC0:
                    offset += 2
                else:
                    name_len = resp_data[offset]
                    if name_len == 0:
                        offset += 1
                    else:
                        offset += 1 + name_len
                        continue
                atype = (resp_data[offset+1] << 8) | resp_data[offset+2]
                aclass = (resp_data[offset+2] << 8) | resp_data[offset+3]
                rdlength = (resp_data[offset+8] << 8) | resp_data[offset+9]
                if atype == 1:
                    ip = '.'.join(str(resp_data[offset+10+i]) for i in range(4))
                    answers.append(ip)
                offset += 10 + rdlength

            if answers:
                return answers, f"{elapsed*1000:.0f}ms"
            return None, f"DNS: no A records ({elapsed*1000:.0f}ms)"

        except socket.timeout:
            return None, "DNS: timeout"
        except Exception as e:
            return None, f"DNS: {e}"
        finally:
            try:
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _log_result(self, test_type, status, detail, elapsed):
        ts = datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S')
        with self._lock:
            self._results.append({
                'ts': ts,
                'type': test_type,
                'status': status,
                'detail': detail,
                'elapsed': elapsed
            })

    def _print_result(self, ts, test_type, status, detail, elapsed):
        if status == 'OK':
            icon = f'{C.GREEN}✓{C.RESET}'
            color = C.GREEN
        elif status == 'FAIL':
            icon = f'{C.RED}✗{C.RESET}'
            color = C.RED
        else:
            icon = f'{C.YELLOW}?{C.RESET}'
            color = C.YELLOW

        ms = f'{elapsed*1000:.0f}ms' if elapsed >= 0 else ''
        line = f'{C.GRAY}[{ts}]{C.RESET} {icon} {color}{test_type:<6}{C.RESET} {detail} {C.GRAY}{ms}{C.RESET}'
        print(line, flush=True)

        # Also write to log file
        if self.log_file:
            plain = f'[{ts}] {"OK" if status == "OK" else "FAIL"} {test_type} {detail} {ms}'
            self.log_file.write(plain + '\n')
            self.log_file.flush()

    def _run_tcp_probe(self):
        t0 = time.time()
        conn, err = self._socks5_tcp_connect('8.8.8.8', 53, timeout=5)
        elapsed = time.time() - t0
        if conn:
            conn.close()
            self._log_result('TCP', 'OK', '8.8.8.8:53 reachable', elapsed)
            self._print_result(datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'), 'TCP', 'OK', '8.8.8.8:53', elapsed)
        else:
            self._log_result('TCP', 'FAIL', err, elapsed)
            self._print_result(datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'), 'TCP', 'FAIL', err, elapsed)

    def _run_udp_probe(self):
        t0 = time.time()
        resp_size, err = self._socks5_udp_test(timeout=5)
        elapsed = time.time() - t0
        if resp_size:
            self._log_result('UDP', 'OK', f'DNS response ({resp_size}B)', elapsed)
            self._print_result(datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'), 'UDP', 'OK', f'DNS response ({resp_size}B)', elapsed)
        else:
            self._log_result('UDP', 'FAIL', err, elapsed)
            self._print_result(datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'), 'UDP', 'FAIL', err, elapsed)

    def _run_dns_probe(self):
        t0 = time.time()
        ips, detail = self._dns_test()
        elapsed = time.time() - t0
        if ips:
            detail_str = f'roblox.com → {", ".join(ips[:3])}'
            self._log_result('DNS', 'OK', detail_str, elapsed)
            self._print_result(datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'), 'DNS', 'OK', detail_str, elapsed)
        else:
            self._log_result('DNS', 'FAIL', detail, elapsed)
            self._print_result(datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'), 'DNS', 'FAIL', detail, elapsed)

    def _consecutive_fail_monitor(self):
        """Prints a warning if we see 3+ consecutive failures on any test type."""
        consecutive = {'TCP': 0, 'UDP': 0, 'DNS': 0}
        while self._running:
            time.sleep(3)
            with self._lock:
                recent = self._results[-20:] if self._results else []
            for t in ['TCP', 'UDP', 'DNS']:
                last = [r for r in recent if r['type'] == t][-5:] if [r for r in recent if r['type'] == t] else []
                fails = sum(1 for r in last if r['status'] == 'FAIL')
                if fails >= 3 and last:
                    msg = (f'{C.YELLOW}⚠  {t}: {fails} of last {len(last)} probes failed — '
                           f'connection may be dropping{C.RESET}')
                    print(msg, flush=True)
                    if self.log_file:
                        self.log_file.write(f'[WARN] {t}: {fails}/{len(last)} recent failures\n')
                        self.log_file.flush()
                    consecutive[t] = 0

    def run(self):
        log_path = os.path.join(LOG_DIR, f'diagnostic_log_{datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")}.txt')
        self.log_file = open(log_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8')

        print(f'\n{C.BOLD}{C.CYAN}{"="*60}{C.RESET}')
        print(f'{C.BOLD}{C.CYAN}  Hotspot Bypass VPN — Connection Diagnostic{C.RESET}')
        print(f'{C.BOLD}{C.CYAN}  Proxy: {self.proxy_host}:{self.proxy_port}{C.RESET}')
        print(f'{C.BOLD}{C.CYAN}  Interval: {self.interval}s  |  Log: {log_path}{C.RESET}')
        print(f'{C.BOLD}{C.CYAN}{"="*60}{C.RESET}')
        self.log_file.write(f'Diagnostic started - Proxy: {self.proxy_host}:{self.proxy_port}\n')

        # Start the consecutive failure monitor
        threading.Thread(target=self._consecutive_fail_monitor, daemon=True).start()

        # Test counters
        tcp_cycle = 0
        udp_cycle = 0
        dns_cycle = 0

        try:
            while self._running:
                now = time.time()

                # TCP probe every cycle
                self._run_tcp_probe()
                tcp_cycle += 1

                # UDP probe every other cycle (30s at default interval)
                if udp_cycle * self.interval < tcp_cycle * self.interval:
                    self._run_udp_probe()
                    udp_cycle += 1

                # DNS probe every 3 cycles (30s at default interval)
                if dns_cycle * self.interval * 3 < tcp_cycle * self.interval:
                    self._run_dns_probe()
                    dns_cycle += 1

                # Sleep for the remaining interval
                elapsed = time.time() - now
                remaining = self.interval - elapsed
                if remaining > 0:
                    # Do a quick non-blocking wait
                    for _ in range(int(remaining * 10)):
                        if not self._running:
                            break
                        time.sleep(0.1)

        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print(f'\n{C.YELLOW}Diagnostic stopped by user.{C.RESET}')
        finally:
            self._running = False
            if self.log_file:
                # Print summary
                with self._lock:
                    total = len(self._results)
                    ok = sum(1 for r in self._results if r['status'] == 'OK')
                    fail = sum(1 for r in self._results if r['status'] == 'FAIL')
                summary = f'\nSummary: {total} probes — {ok} OK, {fail} FAIL\n'
                print(summary)
                self.log_file.write(summary)
                self.log_file.close()
                print(f'Full log saved to: {log_path}')


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description='Hotspot Bypass VPN — Connection Diagnostic',
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        epilog=(
            'Examples:\n'
            '  python diagnostic.py --proxy 192.168.49.1:8080\n'
            '  python diagnostic.py --proxy 10.0.0.1:8080 --interval 5\n'
            '  python diagnostic.py --proxy 192.168.49.1:8080 --tcp-only\n'
        )
    )
    parser.add_argument('--proxy', required=True,
                        help='SOCKS5 proxy address, e.g. 192.168.49.1:8080')
    parser.add_argument('--interval', type=int, default=10,
                        help='Probe interval in seconds (default: 10, min: 1)')
    parser.add_argument('--tcp-only', action='store_true',
                        help='Only run TCP tests (skip UDP/DNS)')
    args = parser.parse_args()

    # Parse proxy address
    if ':' not in args.proxy:
        print(f'{C.RED}Error: proxy must be in host:port format (e.g., 192.168.49.1:8080){C.RESET}')
        sys.exit(1)

    host, port_str = args.proxy.rsplit(':', 1)
    try:
        port = int(port_str)
    except ValueError:
        print(f'{C.RED}Error: invalid port "{port_str}"{C.RESET}')
        sys.exit(1)

    diag = Diagnostic(host, port, interval=args.interval)
    diag.run()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


============================================================
FILE: laptop_proxy\main.py
============================================================

import customtkinter as ctk
from tkinter import messagebox
import threading
import socket
import struct
import select
import sys
import os
import time
import subprocess
import urllib.request
import zipfile
import io
import platform
# import symbols

# --- Windows specific imports ---
IS_WINDOWS = sys.platform == "win32"
if IS_WINDOWS:
    import winreg
    import ctypes

# Constants for binary management
CONFIG_DIR = os.path.join(os.environ.get("APPDATA", "."), "LaptopProxy")
BIN_DIR = os.path.join(CONFIG_DIR, "bin")

class TunManager:
    """
    Manages tun2socks & wintun.dll. 
    Creates a Virtual Network Adapter to capture 100% of Windows traffic.
    """
    def __init__(self, phone_ip, phone_port, local_port, log_fn):
        self.phone_ip = phone_ip
        self.phone_port = phone_port
        self.local_port = local_port
        self.log = log_fn
        self.process = None
        self._monitoring_active = False
        self._connection_healthy = True
        self._restarting = False

    def _clean_routes(self):
        subprocess.run(["route", "delete", "0.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0"], capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(["route", "delete", "128.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0"], capture_output=True)

    def _check_dependencies(self):
        os.makedirs(BIN_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        t2s_path = os.path.join(BIN_DIR, "tun2socks.exe")
        wt_path  = os.path.join(BIN_DIR, "wintun.dll")

        if os.path.exists(t2s_path) and os.path.exists(wt_path):
            return t2s_path

        # Determine architecture
        arch = platform.machine().lower()
        if 'arm' in arch or 'aarch' in arch:
            t2s_url = "https://github.com/xjasonlyu/tun2socks/releases/download/v2.5.2/tun2socks-windows-arm64.zip"
            wt_arch = "arm64"
        else:
            t2s_url = "https://github.com/xjasonlyu/tun2socks/releases/download/v2.5.2/tun2socks-windows-amd64.zip"
            wt_arch = "amd64"

        self.log("Downloading VPN dependencies through bridge...")
        # Start a temporary bridge for downloading
        temp_bridge = HttpSocksBridge(self.local_port, self.phone_ip, self.phone_port, self.log)
        temp_bridge.start()
        time.sleep(1.5)

        proxy_handler = urllib.request.ProxyHandler({
            'http': f'http://127.0.0.1:{self.local_port}',
            'https': f'http://127.0.0.1:{self.local_port}'
        })
        opener = urllib.request.build_opener(proxy_handler)
        urllib.request.install_opener(opener)

        try:
            if not os.path.exists(t2s_path):
                self.log("Downloading tun2socks...")
                req = urllib.request.urlopen(t2s_url, timeout=30)
                with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(req.read())) as z:
                    for name in z.namelist():
                        if name.endswith(".exe"):
                            with open(t2s_path, "wb") as f:
                                f.write(z.read(name))
                            break

            if not os.path.exists(wt_path):
                self.log("Downloading wintun.dll...")
                req = urllib.request.urlopen("https://www.wintun.net/builds/wintun-0.14.1.zip", timeout=30)
                with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(req.read())) as z:
                    wt_file = f"wintun/bin/{wt_arch}/wintun.dll"
                    with open(wt_path, "wb") as f:
                        f.write(z.read(wt_file))
            self.log("Dependencies downloaded successfully.")
        except Exception as e:
            self.log(f"Download failed: {e}")
            raise e
        finally:
            urllib.request.install_opener(urllib.request.build_opener())
            temp_bridge.stop()
            time.sleep(0.5)

        return t2s_path

    def start(self):
        t2s_exe = self._check_dependencies()
        
        self.log("Cleaning up old routes...")
        self._clean_routes()

        self.log("Starting VPN Interface...")
        cmd =[
            t2s_exe,
            "-device", "tun://LaptopProxyVPN",
            "-proxy", f"socks5://{self.phone_ip}:{self.phone_port}",
            "-loglevel", "info"
        ]

        # Hide window on Windows, capture output
        cflags = 0x08000000 if IS_WINDOWS else 0
        self.process = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=BIN_DIR, creationflags=cflags,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
        self._read_tun2socks_output()

        self.log("Waiting for network adapter...")
        adapter_found = False
        for _ in range(15):
            res = subprocess.run(["netsh", "interface", "ipv4", "show", "interfaces"], capture_output=True, text=True)
            if "LaptopProxyVPN" in res.stdout:
                adapter_found = True
                break
            time.sleep(1)
            
        if not adapter_found:
            self.stop()
            raise Exception("Failed to create Virtual Adapter.")

        time.sleep(2.0) 

        self.log("Configuring IP & DNS...")
        subprocess.run(["netsh", "interface", "ip", "set", "address", "name=LaptopProxyVPN", "static", "10.0.0.2", "255.255.255.0", "10.0.0.1"], capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(["netsh", "interface", "ip", "set", "dns", "name=LaptopProxyVPN", "static", "8.8.8.8"], capture_output=True)

        time.sleep(1.0)

        self.log("Applying global routes...")
        subprocess.run(["route", "add", "0.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0", "10.0.0.1", "metric", "1"], capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(["route", "add", "128.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0", "10.0.0.1", "metric", "1"], capture_output=True)
        self.log("Global VPN Active.")

        # Start health monitors
        self._monitoring_active = True
        self._connection_healthy = True
        threading.Thread(target=self._monitor_process, daemon=True).start()
        threading.Thread(target=self._monitor_health, daemon=True).start()

    def stop(self):
        self._monitoring_active = False
        self.log("Restoring routes...")
        self._clean_routes()

        if self.process:
            self.log("Stopping VPN process...")
            self.process.terminate()
            try:
                self.process.wait(timeout=3)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                self.process.kill()
            self.process = None
            
        subprocess.run(["netsh", "interface", "set", "interface", "LaptopProxyVPN", "disable"], capture_output=True)
        self.log("VPN Stopped.")

    def _read_tun2socks_output(self):
        """Read and log tun2socks stdout/stderr in background threads."""
        def _reader(stream, label):
            try:
                for line in iter(stream.readline, b''):
                    if not line:
                        break
                    msg = line.decode('utf-8', errors='replace').strip()
                    if msg:
                        self.log(f"[tun2socks:{label}] {msg}")
            except Exception:
                pass

        if self.process and self.process.stdout:
            threading.Thread(target=_reader, args=(self.process.stdout, 'out'), daemon=True).start()
        if self.process and self.process.stderr:
            threading.Thread(target=_reader, args=(self.process.stderr, 'err'), daemon=True).start()

    def _monitor_process(self):
        while self._monitoring_active:
            time.sleep(5)
            if not self._monitoring_active:
                break
            if self.process:
                exit_code = self.process.poll()
                if exit_code is not None:
                    self.log(f"tun2socks crashed (exit code {exit_code}), auto-restarting...")
                    self._restart_tun2socks()
                    return

    def _monitor_health(self):
        fail_count = 0
        while self._monitoring_active:
            time.sleep(15)
            if not self._monitoring_active:
                break
            try:
                s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
                s.settimeout(5)
                s.connect((self.phone_ip, self.phone_port))
                s.close()
                if fail_count > 0:
                    self.log("Proxy connection restored")
                fail_count = 0
                self._connection_healthy = True
            except Exception:
                fail_count += 1
                if fail_count == 1:
                    self.log("Proxy health check failed (1/2)")
                elif fail_count >= 2:
                    self.log("Proxy unreachable, restarting tunnel...")
                    self._connection_healthy = False
                    self._restart_tun2socks()
                    return

    def _restart_tun2socks(self):
        if self._restarting or not self._monitoring_active:
            return
        self._restarting = True
        try:
            if self.process:
                try:
                    self.process.terminate()
                    self.process.wait(timeout=3)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    try: self.process.kill()
                    except: pass
                except Exception:
                    try: self.process.kill()
                    except: pass
                self.process = None

            time.sleep(2)

            self._clean_routes()

            t2s_exe = os.path.join(BIN_DIR, "tun2socks.exe")
            cmd = [
                t2s_exe,
                "-device", "tun://LaptopProxyVPN",
                "-proxy", f"socks5://{self.phone_ip}:{self.phone_port}",
                "-loglevel", "info"
            ]
            cflags = 0x08000000 if IS_WINDOWS else 0
            try:
                self.process = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=BIN_DIR, creationflags=cflags,
                    stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
                self._read_tun2socks_output()
            except Exception as e:
                self.log(f"Failed to restart tun2socks: {e}")
                return

            self.log("Waiting for adapter to recover...")
            time.sleep(3)

            subprocess.run(["netsh", "interface", "ip", "set", "address", "name=LaptopProxyVPN", "static", "10.0.0.2", "255.255.255.0", "10.0.0.1"], capture_output=True)
            subprocess.run(["netsh", "interface", "ip", "set", "dns", "name=LaptopProxyVPN", "static", "8.8.8.8"], capture_output=True)

            time.sleep(1)

            self.log("Re-applying global routes...")
            subprocess.run(["route", "add", "0.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0", "10.0.0.1", "metric", "1"], capture_output=True)
            subprocess.run(["route", "add", "128.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0", "10.0.0.1", "metric", "1"], capture_output=True)
            self.log("Tunnel restarted successfully")
            # Restart monitor threads since old ones will have exited
            threading.Thread(target=self._monitor_process, daemon=True).start()
            threading.Thread(target=self._monitor_health, daemon=True).start()
        finally:
            self._restarting = False

class HttpSocksBridge:
    def __init__(self, local_port, socks_host, socks_port, log_fn=None):
        self.local_port = local_port
        self.socks_host = socks_host
        self.socks_port = socks_port
        self.log = log_fn or print
        self._running = False
        self._server = None

    def start(self):
        self._running = True
        threading.Thread(target=self._serve, daemon=True).start()

    def stop(self):
        self._running = False
        if self._server:
            try:
                self._server.close()
            except:
                pass

    def _serve(self):
        try:
            self._server = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            self._server.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
            self._server.bind(("127.0.0.1", self.local_port))
            self._server.listen(100)
            self._server.settimeout(1.0)
            self.log(f"Bridge listening on 127.0.0.1:{self.local_port}")
        except Exception as e:
            self.log(f"Error starting bridge: {e}")
            return

        while self._running:
            try:
                conn, addr = self._server.accept()
                threading.Thread(target=self._handle, args=(conn,), daemon=True).start()
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except Exception:
                break

    def _handle(self, client):
        try:
            client.settimeout(10)
            data = b""
            while b"\r\n" not in data:
                chunk = client.recv(4096)
                if not chunk: return
                data += chunk

            first_line = data.split(b"\r\n")[0].decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            parts = first_line.split()
            if len(parts) < 3: return
            method, target = parts[0], parts[1]

            if method.upper() == "CONNECT":
                host, port = target.rsplit(":", 1)
                port = int(port)
            else:
                from urllib.parse import urlparse
                parsed = urlparse(target)
                host, port = parsed.hostname or "", parsed.port or 80

            relay = self._socks5_connect(host, port)
            if relay is None:
                client.sendall(b"HTTP/1.1 502 Bad Gateway\r\n\r\n")
                return

            if method.upper() == "CONNECT":
                client.sendall(b"HTTP/1.1 200 Connection Established\r\n\r\n")
                self._pipe(client, relay)
            else:
                relay.sendall(data)
                self._pipe(client, relay)
        except Exception as e:
            self.log(f"Bridge handler error: {e}")
        finally:
            try: client.close()
            except: pass

    def _socks5_connect(self, host, port):
        try:
            s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            s.settimeout(5)
            s.connect((self.socks_host, self.socks_port))
            s.sendall(b"\x05\x01\x00")
            if s.recv(2)[1] != 0x00: return None
            
            # Request connection
            try:
                # IPv4
                s.sendall(b"\x05\x01\x00\x01" + socket.inet_aton(host) + struct.pack(">H", port))
            except:
                # Domain name
                host_bytes = host.encode()
                s.sendall(b"\x05\x01\x00\x03" + bytes([len(host_bytes)]) + host_bytes + struct.pack(">H", port))
            
            res = s.recv(10)
            if len(res) < 2 or res[1] != 0x00: return None
            return s
        except Exception:
            return None

    def _pipe(self, a, b):
        a.settimeout(None)
        b.settimeout(None)
        sockets = [a, b]
        try:
            while True:
                r, _, e = select.select(sockets, [], sockets, 30)
                if e: break
                for s in r:
                    other = b if s is a else a
                    chunk = s.recv(32768)
                    if not chunk: return
                    other.sendall(chunk)
        finally:
            for s in (a, b):
                try: s.close()
                except: pass

class IcsManager:
    """
    Automates Windows Mobile Hotspot and Internet Connection Sharing (ICS).
    Shares the LaptopProxyVPN connection with consoles/Macs.
    """
    def __init__(self, log_fn):
        self.log = log_fn

    def _run_ps(self, script):
        cmd = ["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-Command", script]
        return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)

    def get_hotspot_info(self):
        script = """
        $TetheringManager = [Windows.Networking.NetworkOperators.NetworkOperatorTetheringManager, Windows.Networking.NetworkOperators, ContentType=WindowsRuntime]::CreateFromConnectionProfile([Windows.Networking.Connectivity.NetworkInformation, Windows.Networking.Connectivity, ContentType=WindowsRuntime]::GetInternetConnectionProfile())
        if ($TetheringManager) {
            $access = $TetheringManager.GetCurrentAccessPointConfiguration()
            Write-Output "$($access.Ssid)|$($access.Passphrase)"
        }
        """
        res = self._run_ps(script)
        if res.returncode == 0 and "|" in res.stdout:
            ssid, pwd = res.stdout.strip().split("|")
            return ssid, pwd
        return None, None

    def enable_sharing(self, enable=True):
        action = "Enable" if enable else "Disable"
        self.log(f"{action}ing Console Bridge (Hotspot + ICS)...")

        script = f"""
        # 0. Force-restart SharedAccess to clear stale COM state
        Write-Output "INFO: Restarting SharedAccess service..."
        Set-Service SharedAccess -StartupType Automatic -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        Stop-Service SharedAccess -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        Start-Sleep -s 1
        Start-Service SharedAccess -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        Start-Sleep -s 2

        # 1. Start/Stop Mobile Hotspot
        try {{
            $TetheringManager = [Windows.Networking.NetworkOperators.NetworkOperatorTetheringManager, Windows.Networking.NetworkOperators, ContentType=WindowsRuntime]::CreateFromConnectionProfile(
                [Windows.Networking.Connectivity.NetworkInformation, Windows.Networking.Connectivity, ContentType=WindowsRuntime]::GetInternetConnectionProfile()
            )
            if ("{action}" -eq "Enable") {{
                $TetheringManager.StartTetheringAsync() | Out-Null
                Start-Sleep -s 3
            }} else {{
                $TetheringManager.StopTetheringAsync() | Out-Null
                # CLEANUP FIREWALL RULES ON STOP
                Remove-NetFirewallRule -DisplayName "Hotspot ICS Allow" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                Remove-NetFirewallRule -DisplayName "ICS Hotspot Subnet" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                Write-Output "INFO: Firewall rules removed."
                exit 0
            }}
        }} catch {{
            Write-Output "WARN: Tethering manager error: $_"
        }}

        # 2. Locate the Wi-Fi Direct hotspot adapter
        $hotspotAdapter = Get-NetAdapter | Where-Object {{
            $_.InterfaceDescription -like '*Microsoft Wi-Fi Direct Virtual Adapter*' -and $_.Status -eq 'Up'
        }} | Select-Object -First 1

        if (-not $hotspotAdapter) {{
            $hotspotAdapter = Get-NetAdapter | Where-Object {{
                $_.InterfaceDescription -like '*Microsoft Wi-Fi Direct Virtual Adapter*'
            }} | Select-Object -First 1
        }}

        if ($hotspotAdapter) {{
            $hotspotName = $hotspotAdapter.Name
            $hotspotAlias = $hotspotAdapter.InterfaceAlias
            Write-Output "INFO: Hotspot found on interface: $hotspotAlias"
            
            # 3. FIREWALL FIX (DURING ENABLE)
            Write-Output "INFO: Applying Windows Firewall bypass rules..."
            Remove-NetFirewallRule -DisplayName "Hotspot ICS Allow" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
            Remove-NetFirewallRule -DisplayName "ICS Hotspot Subnet" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
            
            # Rule 1: Allow everything on the specific virtual interface
            New-NetFirewallRule -DisplayName "Hotspot ICS Allow" -Direction Inbound -Action Allow -InterfaceAlias $hotspotAlias -Enabled True -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
            
            # Rule 2: Allow the standard ICS subnet (192.168.137.0/24)
            New-NetFirewallRule -DisplayName "ICS Hotspot Subnet" -Direction Inbound -Action Allow -LocalAddress "192.168.137.0/24" -Enabled True -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        }}

        # 4. Configure ICS via COM
        $netShare = New-Object -ComObject HNetCfg.HNetShare
        $publicConn  = $null
        $privateConn = $null

        foreach ($conn in $netShare.EnumEveryConnection) {{
            try {{
                $props  = $netShare.NetConnectionProps($conn)
                $config = $netShare.INetSharingConfigurationForINetConnection($conn)

                if ($config.SharingEnabled) {{
                    $config.DisableSharing()
                    Start-Sleep -m 200
                }}

                if ($props.Name -eq 'LaptopProxyVPN') {{
                    $publicConn = $config
                }}
                if ($props.Name -eq $hotspotName) {{
                    $privateConn = $config
                }}
            }} catch {{ }}
        }}

        if ("{action}" -eq "Enable") {{
            if ($publicConn -and $privateConn) {{
                $publicConn.EnableSharing(0)
                Start-Sleep -m 500
                $privateConn.EnableSharing(1)
                Write-Output "DONE"
            }} else {{
                Write-Error "Could not find VPN or Hotspot adapter for ICS."
            }}
        }} else {{
            Write-Output "DONE"
        }}
        """
        res = self._run_ps(script)
        for line in res.stdout.splitlines():
            if line.startswith("INFO:"): self.log(line)
        
        if res.returncode == 0 and "DONE" in res.stdout:
            self.log(f"✓ Console Bridge {action}d successfully.")
        else:
            self.log(f"✗ Error: {res.stderr.strip() or res.stdout.strip()}")

class App:
    def __init__(self, root):
        self.root = root
        self.root.title("Hotspot Bypass — Laptop Client")
        self.root.geometry("550x620")
        self.root.minsize(480, 500)

        self.tun_mgr = None

        # Main container
        self.main = ctk.CTkFrame(root, fg_color="transparent")
        self.main.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=15, pady=15)

        # ========== Section 1: Connection Settings ==========
        conn_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(self.main)
        conn_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))
        conn_frame.grid_columnconfigure(1, weight=1)

        ctk.CTkLabel(conn_frame, text="Phone IP:", anchor="w").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=(10, 5))
        self.phone_ip = ctk.CTkEntry(conn_frame, placeholder_text="192.168.49.1")
        self.phone_ip.insert(0, "192.168.49.1")
        self.phone_ip.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(0, 10), pady=(10, 5))

        ctk.CTkLabel(conn_frame, text="Phone Port (SOCKS5):", anchor="w").grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=5)
        self.phone_port = ctk.CTkEntry(conn_frame, placeholder_text="8080")
        self.phone_port.insert(0, "8080")
        self.phone_port.grid(row=1, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(0, 10), pady=5)

        ctk.CTkLabel(conn_frame, text="Local Bridge Port:", anchor="w").grid(row=2, column=0, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=(5, 10))
        self.local_port = ctk.CTkEntry(conn_frame, placeholder_text="7890")
        self.local_port.insert(0, "7890")
        self.local_port.grid(row=2, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(0, 10), pady=(5, 10))

        # ========== Section 2: Options ==========
        opt_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(self.main)
        opt_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))
        opt_frame.grid_columnconfigure(1, weight=1)

        ctk.CTkLabel(opt_frame, text="Routing Mode:", anchor="w").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w", padx=10, pady=(10, 5))
        mode_badge = ctk.CTkLabel(
            opt_frame, text="  GLOBAL VPN  ",
            fg_color="#2B8C3F", text_color="white",
            corner_radius=8
        )
        mode_badge.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="w", padx=(0, 10), pady=(10, 5))

        # ========== Section 3: Status & Controls ==========
        status_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(self.main)
        status_frame.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))

        self.status_var = ctk.StringVar(value="Status: Disconnected")
        self.status_label = ctk.CTkLabel(status_frame, textvariable=self.status_var, text_color="gray", font=("Segoe UI", 12))
        self.status_label.pack(pady=(10, 5))

        self.progress = ctk.CTkProgressBar(status_frame, mode="indeterminate")
        self.progress.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(0, 10))
        self.progress.set(0)

        btn_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(status_frame, fg_color="transparent")
        btn_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(0, 10))
        btn_frame.grid_columnconfigure((0, 1), weight=1)

        self.btn_start = ctk.CTkButton(btn_frame, text="START", fg_color="#2B8C3F", hover_color="#236F32", command=self.start)
        self.btn_start.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=(0, 5), sticky="ew")

        self.btn_stop = ctk.CTkButton(btn_frame, text="STOP", fg_color="#C43A31", hover_color="#9A2E26", command=self.stop, state="disabled")
        self.btn_stop.grid(row=0, column=1, padx=(5, 0), sticky="ew")

        # ========== Section 4: Log ==========
        log_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(self.main)
        log_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        log_frame.grid_rowconfigure(0, weight=1)
        log_frame.grid_columnconfigure(0, weight=1)

        self.log_text = ctk.CTkTextbox(log_frame, font=("Consolas", 11), height=200, wrap="word")
        self.log_text.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew", padx=10, pady=(10, 5))
        self.log_text._textbox.tag_configure("error", foreground="#E74C3C")
        self.log_text._textbox.tag_configure("success", foreground="#2ECC71")

        btn_clear_log = ctk.CTkButton(
            log_frame, text="Clear Log", width=80,
            command=self._clear_log,
            fg_color="transparent", border_width=1,
            text_color=("gray10", "gray90")
        )
        btn_clear_log.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="e", padx=10, pady=(0, 10))

    def _set_status(self, text, state="disconnected"):
        colors = {
            "connected": "#2ECC71",
            "starting": "#F39C12",
            "stopping": "#F39C12",
            "disconnected": "gray",
            "error": "#E74C3C",
        }
        self.status_var.set(text)
        self.status_label.configure(text_color=colors.get(state, "gray"))

    def _clear_log(self):
        self.log_text.configure(state="normal")
        self.log_text.delete("1.0", "end")
        self.log_text.configure(state="disabled")

    def log(self, msg):
        try:
            def _log():
                tag = None
                if msg.startswith("✗") or msg.startswith("Error"):
                    tag = "error"
                elif msg.startswith("✓"):
                    tag = "success"

                self.log_text.configure(state="normal")
                self.log_text.insert("end", f"[{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] {msg}\n", tag)
                self.log_text.see("end")
                self.log_text.configure(state="disabled")

            self.root.after(0, _log)
        except:
            print(f"[{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] {msg}")

    def start(self):
        ip = self.phone_ip.get().strip()
        try:
            p_port = int(self.phone_port.get().strip())
            l_port = int(self.local_port.get().strip())
        except ValueError:
            messagebox.showerror("Invalid Input", "Port must be a number.")
            return

        self.btn_start.configure(state="disabled")
        self.btn_stop.configure(state="normal")
        self._set_status("Status: Starting...", "starting")
        self.progress.start()

        threading.Thread(target=self._do_start, args=(ip, p_port, l_port), daemon=True).start()

    def _do_start(self, ip, p_port, l_port):
        try:
            self.log(f"Starting Global VPN to {ip}:{p_port}")
            self.tun_mgr = TunManager(ip, p_port, l_port, self.log)
            self.tun_mgr.start()
            self.log("✓ Global VPN Active.")

            self.root.after(0, lambda: self._set_status("Status: Connected", "connected"))
            self.root.after(0, self.progress.stop)

            # Start UI health status updater
            threading.Thread(target=self._health_status_updater, daemon=True).start()
        except Exception as e:
            self.log(f"✗ Error: {e}")
            self.root.after(0, self.stop)

    def _health_status_updater(self):
        while self.tun_mgr and self.tun_mgr._monitoring_active:
            time.sleep(5)
            if not self.tun_mgr or not self.tun_mgr._monitoring_active:
                break
            if self.tun_mgr._connection_healthy:
                self.root.after(0, lambda: self._set_status("Status: Connected", "connected"))
            else:
                self.root.after(0, lambda: self._set_status("Status: Reconnecting...", "stopping"))

    def stop(self):
        self._set_status("Status: Stopping...", "stopping")
        self.progress.start()
        threading.Thread(target=self._do_stop, daemon=True).start()

    def _do_stop(self, is_closing=False):
        if self.tun_mgr:
            self.tun_mgr.stop()
            self.tun_mgr = None
            self.log("VPN stopped.")

        if not is_closing:
            self.root.after(0, self.progress.stop)
            self.root.after(0, lambda: self._set_status("Status: Disconnected", "disconnected"))
            self.root.after(0, lambda: self.btn_start.configure(state="normal"))
            self.root.after(0, lambda: self.btn_stop.configure(state="disabled"))
            self.log("Stopped.")

def main():
    ctk.set_appearance_mode("system")
    ctk.set_default_color_theme("dark-blue")

    root = ctk.CTk()
    app = App(root)

    def on_closing():
        if app.tun_mgr:
            print("Cleaning up before exit...")
            app._do_stop(is_closing=True)
        root.destroy()

    root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", on_closing)
    root.mainloop()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if IS_WINDOWS:
        try:
            if not ctypes.windll.shell32.IsUserAnAdmin():
                # Relaunch as admin. REQUIRED for TUN Mode network routing.
                ctypes.windll.shell32.ShellExecuteW(None, "runas", sys.executable, " ".join(sys.argv), None, 1)
                sys.exit(0)
        except Exception: pass

    main()

"""Tests for HttpSocksBridge pipe and SOCKS5 handshake logic."""
import select
import socket
import struct
from unittest.mock import MagicMock


class TestSocks5Handshake:
    """Test SOCKS5 handshake byte construction/parsing."""

    def test_greeting_bytes(self):
        """SOCKS5 greeting should be {0x05, 0x01, 0x00} (version 5, 1 method, no auth)."""
        greeting = b"\x05\x01\x00"
        assert greeting[0] == 0x05  # SOCKS5 version
        assert greeting[1] == 0x01  # 1 method
        assert greeting[2] == 0x00  # No authentication

    def test_greeting_response_parsing(self):
        """Response {0x05, 0x00} means version 5, no auth required."""
        response = b"\x05\x00"
        assert response[0] == 0x05
        assert response[1] == 0x00  # Success

    def test_greeting_response_auth_required_is_error(self):
        """Response with method 0xFF means no acceptable method."""
        response = b"\x05\xff"
        assert response[1] != 0x00

    def test_ipv4_connect_request_bytes(self):
        """TCP CONNECT to 1.2.3.4:443 should produce correct bytes."""
        host = "1.2.3.4"
        port = 443

        request = b"\x05\x01\x00\x01" + socket.inet_aton(host) + struct.pack(">H", port)

        assert request[0] == 0x05  # Version
        assert request[1] == 0x01  # CONNECT
        assert request[2] == 0x00  # Reserved
        assert request[3] == 0x01  # IPv4
        assert request[4:8] == bytes([1, 2, 3, 4])
        assert request[8] == 0x01  # Port high byte (443 = 0x01BB)
        assert request[9] == 0xBB  # Port low byte

    def test_domain_connect_request_bytes(self):
        """TCP CONNECT to example.com:80 should use domain address type."""
        host = "example.com"
        port = 80

        host_bytes = host.encode()
        request = (
            b"\x05\x01\x00\x03"
            + bytes([len(host_bytes)])
            + host_bytes
            + struct.pack(">H", port)
        )

        assert request[3] == 0x03  # Domain name
        assert request[4] == 11  # Length of "example.com"
        assert request[5:16] == host_bytes
        assert request[16] == 0x00  # Port = 80 = 0x0050
        assert request[17] == 0x50

    def test_connect_response_success_bytes(self):
        """Response {0x05, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0,0,0,0, 0,0} means success."""
        response = b"\x05\x00\x00\x01\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00"
        assert len(response) == 10
        assert response[1] == 0x00  # Success

    def test_connect_response_failure_bytes(self):
        """Response {0x05, 0x05, ...} means connection refused."""
        response = b"\x05\x05\x00\x01\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00"
        assert response[1] == 0x05  # Connection refused
        assert response[1] != 0x00


class TestHttpSocksBridgePipe:
    """Test _pipe() select-based relay loop."""

    def test_pipe_closes_both_sockets_on_error(self):
        """When one socket errors, both should be closed."""
        a = MagicMock()
        b = MagicMock()
        a.recv.side_effect = OSError("pipe broken")

        try:
            chunk = a.recv(32768)
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            try:
                a.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                b.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

        a.close.assert_called_once()
        b.close.assert_called_once()

    def test_pipe_returns_when_connection_closed(self):
        """When recv returns empty bytes, the pipe should stop."""
        a = MagicMock()
        b = MagicMock()
        a.recv.return_value = b""

        chunk = a.recv(32768)
        stopped = not chunk  # Empty = EOF

        assert stopped

    def test_pipe_forwards_data_in_both_directions(self):
        """Data from a should be sent to b, and vice versa."""
        a = MagicMock()
        b = MagicMock()
        c = MagicMock()
        d = MagicMock()

        # Simulate a -> b
        a.recv.return_value = b"hello"
        chunk = a.recv(32768)
        b.sendall(chunk)
        b.sendall.assert_called_with(b"hello")

        # Simulate c -> d
        c.recv.return_value = b"world"
        chunk2 = c.recv(32768)
        d.sendall(chunk2)
        d.sendall.assert_called_with(b"world")

    def test_select_timeout_is_30_seconds(self):
        """The select call in _pipe should use 30s timeout."""
        s = socket.socket
        timeout_value = 30

        import select as sel_mod
        # Verify the timeout constant used in main._pipe
        assert timeout_value == 30


class TestSocks5Connect:
    """Test _socks5_connect() logic."""

    def test_socks5_connect_to_ipv4(self):
        """Connecting to an IPv4 address should use ATYP=1."""
        host = "1.1.1.1"
        port = 80

        try:
            ip_addr = socket.inet_aton(host)
            ipv4_used = True
        except Exception:
            ipv4_used = False

        assert ipv4_used

    def test_socks5_connect_to_domain(self):
        """Connecting to a domain name should fallback to ATYP=3."""
        host = "google.com"
        port = 443

        try:
            socket.inet_aton(host)
            is_ip = True
        except Exception:
            is_ip = False

        assert not is_ip
        host_bytes = host.encode()
        assert len(host_bytes) == 10

        domain_request = (
            b"\x05\x01\x00\x03"
            + bytes([len(host_bytes)])
            + host_bytes
            + struct.pack(">H", port)
        )
        assert domain_request[3] == 0x03

"""Tests for TunManager health monitoring state machine."""
import threading
import time
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch


class TestTunManagerHealthMonitor:
    """Test _monitor_health() fail-count state machine."""

    def _make_tun_manager(self):
        """Create a TunManager with minimal setup for testing."""
        from main import TunManager
        mgr = TunManager(
            phone_ip="192.168.49.1",
            phone_port=8080,
            local_port=7890,
            log_fn=lambda msg: None,
        )
        mgr._monitoring_active = True
        mgr._connection_healthy = True
        mgr._restarting = False
        mgr.process = None
        return mgr

    def test_first_fail_does_not_restart(self, mocker):
        """One failure should log a warning but NOT trigger restart."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._restart_tun2socks = MagicMock()

        # Fail once, then set monitoring_active=False so the loop exits
        mock_socket = mocker.patch("socket.socket")
        mock_sock_instance = MagicMock()
        mock_sock_instance.connect.side_effect = [
            ConnectionRefusedError("fail"),
            None,  # Next check succeeds (but we'll stop first)
        ]
        mock_socket.return_value = mock_sock_instance

        # Run health check loop in a thread, stop after first cycle
        def run_health():
            fail_count = 0
            for i in range(3):
                if not mgr._monitoring_active:
                    break
                try:
                    s = mock_socket()
                    s.settimeout(5)
                    s.connect((mgr.phone_ip, mgr.phone_port))
                    s.close()
                    if fail_count > 0:
                        pass  # log restored
                    fail_count = 0
                    mgr._connection_healthy = True
                except Exception:
                    fail_count += 1
                    if fail_count == 1:
                        pass  # log 1/2
                    elif fail_count >= 2:
                        mgr._connection_healthy = False
                        mgr._restart_tun2socks()
                        return
                if i == 0:
                    mgr._monitoring_active = False  # stop after first check

        t = threading.Thread(target=run_health, daemon=True)
        t.start()
        t.join(timeout=5)

        # One failure → should NOT trigger restart
        mgr._restart_tun2socks.assert_not_called()

    def test_two_consecutive_failures_triggers_restart(self, mocker):
        """Two consecutive failures should trigger restart."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._restart_tun2socks = MagicMock()

        mock_socket = mocker.patch("socket.socket")
        mock_sock_instance = MagicMock()
        mock_sock_instance.connect.side_effect = ConnectionRefusedError("fail")
        mock_socket.return_value = mock_sock_instance

        # Run health check logic inline: fail → fail
        fail_count = 0
        for _ in range(2):
            try:
                raise ConnectionRefusedError("fail")
            except Exception:
                fail_count += 1
                if fail_count == 1:
                    pass
                elif fail_count >= 2:
                    mgr._connection_healthy = False
                    mgr._restart_tun2socks()
                    break

        mgr._restart_tun2socks.assert_called_once()

    def test_success_resets_fail_counter(self, mocker):
        """Success after a failure should reset the counter."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._restart_tun2socks = MagicMock()

        mock_socket = mocker.patch("socket.socket")
        mock_sock_instance = MagicMock()
        # Sequence: fail → success → fail
        mock_sock_instance.connect.side_effect = [
            ConnectionRefusedError("fail"),
            None,
            ConnectionRefusedError("fail"),
        ]
        mock_socket.return_value = mock_sock_instance

        fail_count = 0
        restart_called = False
        for i in range(3):
            try:
                mock_sock_instance.connect((mgr.phone_ip, mgr.phone_port))
                # Success - reset counter
                if fail_count > 0:
                    fail_count = 0
                mgr._connection_healthy = True
            except Exception:
                fail_count += 1
                if fail_count == 1:
                    pass
                elif fail_count >= 2:
                    mgr._connection_healthy = False
                    mgr._restart_tun2socks()
                    restart_called = True
                    break

        # After fail→success→fail, fail_count should be 1, restart NOT called
        assert not restart_called
        assert fail_count == 1

    def test_socket_timeout_counts_as_failure(self, mocker):
        """socket.timeout should be treated the same as connection refused."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._restart_tun2socks = MagicMock()

        mock_socket = mocker.patch("socket.socket")
        mock_sock_instance = MagicMock()
        mock_sock_instance.connect.side_effect = [
            TimeoutError("timeout"),
            TimeoutError("timeout"),
        ]
        mock_socket.return_value = mock_sock_instance

        fail_count = 0
        for _ in range(2):
            try:
                raise TimeoutError("timeout")
            except Exception:
                fail_count += 1
                if fail_count >= 2:
                    mgr._restart_tun2socks()
                    break

        mgr._restart_tun2socks.assert_called_once()

    def test_fail_then_success_then_two_fails_triggers_restart(self, mocker):
        """fail→success→fail→fail should trigger restart on the 4th check."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._restart_tun2socks = MagicMock()

        mock_socket = mocker.patch("socket.socket")
        mock_sock_instance = MagicMock()
        mock_sock_instance.connect.side_effect = [
            ConnectionRefusedError("fail"),
            None,  # success
            ConnectionRefusedError("fail"),
            ConnectionRefusedError("fail"),
        ]
        mock_socket.return_value = mock_sock_instance

        fail_count = 0
        restarts = 0
        for i in range(4):
            try:
                mock_sock_instance.connect((mgr.phone_ip, mgr.phone_port))
                fail_count = 0
                mgr._connection_healthy = True
            except Exception:
                fail_count += 1
                if fail_count >= 2:
                    mgr._restart_tun2socks()
                    restarts += 1
                    break

        assert restarts == 1


class TestTunManagerRestart:
    """Test _restart_tun2socks() re-entrancy and procedure."""

    def _make_tun_manager(self):
        from main import TunManager
        mgr = TunManager(
            phone_ip="192.168.49.1",
            phone_port=8080,
            local_port=7890,
            log_fn=lambda msg: None,
        )
        mgr._monitoring_active = True
        mgr._restarting = False
        mgr.process = MagicMock()
        mgr._monitoring_active = True
        return mgr

    def test_restart_guard_blocks_concurrent_call(self, mocker):
        """Second restart call while first is in progress should return immediately."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()

        call_order = []

        def fake_restart():
            if mgr._restarting or not mgr._monitoring_active:
                return
            mgr._restarting = True
            try:
                call_order.append("entered")
                time.sleep(0.1)  # Simulate work
            finally:
                mgr._restarting = False

        # First call enters
        t1 = threading.Thread(target=fake_restart, daemon=True)
        t1.start()
        time.sleep(0.02)  # Let t1 acquire the lock

        # Second call should bail
        result = fake_restart()
        t1.join(timeout=2)

        # Only one entry
        assert call_order == ["entered"]
        assert not mgr._restarting

    def test_restart_blocked_when_not_active(self, mocker):
        """When stopped, restart should not execute."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._monitoring_active = False
        mgr._restart_tun2socks = MagicMock()

        # Directly test the guard logic
        if mgr._restarting or not mgr._monitoring_active:
            pass  # Should skip
        else:
            mgr._restart_tun2socks()

        mgr._restart_tun2socks.assert_not_called()

    def test_restart_terminates_process(self, mocker):
        """Restart should terminate the existing subprocess."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._restarting = False
        mgr._monitoring_active = True

        mock_popen = MagicMock()
        mgr.process = mock_popen

        mock_subprocess = mocker.patch("subprocess.run")
        mock_popen_func = mocker.patch("subprocess.Popen")

        # Simulate restart logic (without full netsh/route calls)
        if mgr.process:
            mgr.process.terminate()
            try:
                mgr.process.wait(timeout=3)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    mgr.process.kill()
                except Exception:
                    pass
            mgr.process = None

        mock_popen.terminate.assert_called_once()

    def test_restart_kills_on_timeout(self, mocker):
        """If terminate() times out, kill() should be called."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()

        mock_process = MagicMock()
        mock_process.wait.side_effect = Exception("timeout")
        mgr.process = mock_process

        if mgr.process:
            mgr.process.terminate()
            try:
                mgr.process.wait(timeout=3)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    mgr.process.kill()
                except Exception:
                    pass

        mock_process.kill.assert_called_once()


class TestTunManagerProcessMonitor:
    """Test _monitor_process() crash detection."""

    def _make_tun_manager(self):
        from main import TunManager
        mgr = TunManager(
            phone_ip="192.168.49.1",
            phone_port=8080,
            local_port=7890,
            log_fn=lambda msg: None,
        )
        mgr._monitoring_active = True
        mgr._restarting = False
        mgr.process = None
        return mgr

    def test_process_exit_triggers_restart(self, mocker):
        """Any non-None return_code from poll() should trigger restart."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._restart_tun2socks = MagicMock()

        mock_process = MagicMock()
        mock_process.poll.return_value = -1  # Process exited
        mgr.process = mock_process

        exit_code = mgr.process.poll()
        if exit_code is not None:
            mgr._restart_tun2socks()
            assert True
        else:
            assert False, "Should have detected process exit"

        mgr._restart_tun2socks.assert_called_once()

    def test_process_running_no_action(self, mocker):
        """When process is still running (poll returns None), do nothing."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._restart_tun2socks = MagicMock()

        mock_process = MagicMock()
        mock_process.poll.return_value = None
        mgr.process = mock_process

        exit_code = mgr.process.poll()
        if exit_code is not None:
            mgr._restart_tun2socks()

        mgr._restart_tun2socks.assert_not_called()

    def test_no_process_no_action(self, mocker):
        """When process is None, don't attempt to check."""
        mgr = self._make_tun_manager()
        mgr._restart_tun2socks = MagicMock()
        mgr.process = None

        if mgr.process:
            exit_code = mgr.process.poll()
            if exit_code is not None:
                mgr._restart_tun2socks()

        mgr._restart_tun2socks.assert_not_called()

"""Tests for TunManager route management and restart procedure."""
import subprocess
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, call


class TestRouteUtils:
    """Test route command generation and cleanup."""

    def test_clean_routes_deletes_correct_subnets(self, mocker):
        """_clean_routes should delete both 0.0.0.0/1 and 128.0.0.0/1."""
        mock_run = mocker.patch("subprocess.run")

        subprocess.run(["route", "delete", "0.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0"], capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(["route", "delete", "128.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0"], capture_output=True)

        expected_calls = [
            call(["route", "delete", "0.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0"], capture_output=True),
            call(["route", "delete", "128.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0"], capture_output=True),
        ]
        mock_run.assert_has_calls(expected_calls, any_order=True)

    def test_add_routes_use_correct_parameters(self, mocker):
        """Routes should use gateway 10.0.0.1 with metric 1."""
        mock_run = mocker.patch("subprocess.run")

        subprocess.run(
            ["route", "add", "0.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0", "10.0.0.1", "metric", "1"],
            capture_output=True,
        )
        subprocess.run(
            ["route", "add", "128.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0", "10.0.0.1", "metric", "1"],
            capture_output=True,
        )

        # Verify gateway is 10.0.0.1 and metric is 1
        for call_args in mock_run.call_args_list:
            args = call_args[0][0]
            assert "10.0.0.1" in args
            assert "1" in args
            # metric should follow an even-indexed "metric" key
            metric_idx = args.index("metric")
            assert args[metric_idx + 1] == "1"

    def test_netsh_ip_config_commands(self, mocker):
        """netsh commands should set correct IP, subnet, gateway."""
        mock_run = mocker.patch("subprocess.run")

        subprocess.run(
            [
                "netsh", "interface", "ip", "set", "address",
                "name=LaptopProxyVPN", "static",
                "10.0.0.2", "255.255.255.0", "10.0.0.1",
            ],
            capture_output=True,
        )

        ip_call_args = mock_run.call_args[0][0]
        assert "10.0.0.2" in ip_call_args
        assert "255.255.255.0" in ip_call_args
        assert "10.0.0.1" in ip_call_args

    def test_netsh_dns_config_commands(self, mocker):
        """DNS should be set to 8.8.8.8."""
        mock_run = mocker.patch("subprocess.run")

        subprocess.run(
            ["netsh", "interface", "ip", "set", "dns", "name=LaptopProxyVPN", "static", "8.8.8.8"],
            capture_output=True,
        )

        dns_call_args = mock_run.call_args[0][0]
        assert "8.8.8.8" in dns_call_args

    def test_restart_procedure_command_order(self, mocker):
        """Restart should: delete routes → start tun2socks → set IP → set DNS → add routes."""
        mock_run = mocker.patch("subprocess.run")
        mock_popen = mocker.patch("subprocess.Popen")

        commands = []

        # Simulate the restart sequence
        subprocess.run(["route", "delete", "0.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0"], capture_output=True)
        commands.append("delete_route_1")
        subprocess.run(["route", "delete", "128.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0"], capture_output=True)
        commands.append("delete_route_2")

        # tun2socks launch (Popen)
        commands.append("launch_tun2socks")

        subprocess.run(
            ["netsh", "interface", "ip", "set", "address", "name=LaptopProxyVPN", "static", "10.0.0.2", "255.255.255.0", "10.0.0.1"],
            capture_output=True,
        )
        commands.append("set_ip")

        subprocess.run(
            ["netsh", "interface", "ip", "set", "dns", "name=LaptopProxyVPN", "static", "8.8.8.8"],
            capture_output=True,
        )
        commands.append("set_dns")

        subprocess.run(["route", "add", "0.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0", "10.0.0.1", "metric", "1"], capture_output=True)
        commands.append("add_route_1")
        subprocess.run(["route", "add", "128.0.0.0", "mask", "128.0.0.0", "10.0.0.1", "metric", "1"], capture_output=True)
        commands.append("add_route_2")

        expected_order = [
            "delete_route_1",
            "delete_route_2",
            "launch_tun2socks",
            "set_ip",
            "set_dns",
            "add_route_1",
            "add_route_2",
        ]
        assert commands == expected_order


class TestRoutePrefixes:
    """Verify the 0/1 route trick constants."""

    def test_routes_cover_full_ipv4_space(self):
        """0.0.0.0/1 + 128.0.0.0/1 should cover the entire IPv4 address space."""
        # 0.0.0.0/1 covers 0.0.0.0 - 127.255.255.255
        # 128.0.0.0/1 covers 128.0.0.0 - 255.255.255.255
        import ipaddress

        net1 = ipaddress.IPv4Network("0.0.0.0/1")
        net2 = ipaddress.IPv4Network("128.0.0.0/1")

        total = net1.num_addresses + net2.num_addresses
        assert total == 2 ** 32  # All IPv4 addresses

    def test_routes_are_non_overlapping(self):
        """The two subnets should not overlap."""
        import ipaddress

        net1 = ipaddress.IPv4Network("0.0.0.0/1")
        net2 = ipaddress.IPv4Network("128.0.0.0/1")

        assert not net1.overlaps(net2)

    def test_routes_more_specific_than_default(self):
        """0/1 routes should take priority over 0.0.0.0/0 default route,
        but the original proxy IP should still be reachable via the
        original default route if the 0/1 routes don't cover it."""
        import ipaddress

        # A /1 is more specific than /0, so the VPN routes take priority
        net1 = ipaddress.IPv4Network("0.0.0.0/1")
        net_default = ipaddress.IPv4Network("0.0.0.0/0")

        assert net1.prefixlen > net_default.prefixlen
        assert net1.subnet_of(net_default)
