"""
Hotspot Bypass VPN — Traffic Monitor

Tracks every connection through the proxy in real time by tapping into
ADB logcat on the phone (for target IPs) + Windows adapter stats.

Requires:
  - Phone connected via USB with USB Debugging enabled
  - Main laptop app running and connected (Global VPN)

Usage:
    python traffic_monitor.py
    python traffic_monitor.py --log-dir ./logs
    python traffic_monitor.py --no-adb    (ADB-only mode)
"""

import argparse
import csv
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
DEFAULT_LOG_DIR = os.path.join(HERE, "logs")

# Color support
class Color:
    GREEN = '\033[92m'
    RED = '\033[91m'
    YELLOW = '\033[93m'
    CYAN = '\033[96m'
    GRAY = '\033[90m'
    MAGENTA = '\033[95m'
    BOLD = '\033[1m'
    RESET = '\033[0m'

    @classmethod
    def enabled(cls):
        return hasattr(sys.stdout, 'isatty') and sys.stdout.isatty()

C = Color if Color.enabled() else type('', (), {k: '' for k in vars(Color) if not k.startswith('_') and k != 'enabled'})()


class AdbMonitor:
    """Reads ProxyServer log lines from ADB logcat in real time."""

    LINE_PATTERNS = {
        'connect': re.compile(
            r'\[([^\]]+)\]\s+✓\s+TCP Connected to (.+?):(\d+)'
        ),
        'connect_fail': re.compile(
            r'\[([^\]]+)\]\s+(?:✗\s+)?TCP Connection failed:?\s*(.*)'
        ),
        'transfer': re.compile(
            r'\[([^\]]+)\]\s+(C->T|T->C)\s+transferred\s+(\d+)KB'
        ),
        'pipe_closed': re.compile(
            r'\[([^\]]+)\]\s+(C->T|T->C)\s+pipe closed:\s*(.+)'
        ),
        'closed': re.compile(
            r'\[([^\]]+)\]\s+Connection closed\s+\(Active:\s+(\d+)\)'
        ),
        'udp_associate': re.compile(
            r'\[([^\]]+)\]\s+✓\s+UDP ASSOCIATE on port (\d+)'
        ),
        'udp_cleanup': re.compile(
            r'Cleaned up stale UDP relay:\s*(\S+)'
        ),
        'server_start': re.compile(
            r'✓ PROXY SERVER STARTED'
        ),
        'server_stop': re.compile(
            r'Stopping server'
        ),
    }

    def __init__(self, log_fn, on_connection=None):
        self.log = log_fn
        self.on_connection = on_connection
        self._running = False
        self._proc = None
        self._connections = {}  # clientId -> {target, port, start, up, down}

    def start(self):
        self._running = True
        threading.Thread(target=self._run_logcat, daemon=True).start()

    def stop(self):
        self._running = False
        if self._proc:
            try:
                self._proc.terminate()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _run_logcat(self):
        # Clear old logs first
        subprocess.run(['adb', 'logcat', '-c'], capture_output=True, timeout=5)

        cmd = ['adb', 'logcat', '-s', 'ProxyServer:D', '-v', 'threadtime']
        try:
            self._proc = subprocess.Popen(
                cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                bufsize=0
            )
        except FileNotFoundError:
            self.log('ADB', 'FAIL', 'adb.exe not found. Install Android platform tools or use --no-adb')
            return

        self.log('ADB', 'OK', 'Listening to phone proxy logs...')

        raw_re = re.compile(
            rb'^(\d{2}-\d{2}\s+\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}\.\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+\d+\s+[DIWEF]\s+ProxyServer:\s+(.*)'
        )

        buf = b''
        while self._running:
            try:
                chunk = self._proc.stdout.read(8192)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                buf += chunk
                while b'\n' in buf:
                    line, buf = buf.split(b'\n', 1)
                    line = line.strip()
                    if not line:
                        continue
                    m = raw_re.match(line)
                    if not m:
                        continue
                    ts = m.group(1).decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                    body = m.group(3).decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                    self._parse_line(ts, body)
            except Exception:
                break

    def _parse_line(self, ts, body):
        # TCP Connect
        m = self.LINE_PATTERNS['connect'].search(body)
        if m:
            cid, host, port_s = m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3)
            self._connections[cid] = {
                'target': host, 'port': int(port_s),
                'start': ts, 'up': 0, 'down': 0
            }
            conn = self._connections[cid]
            self.log(
                'ADB', 'CONN',
                f'{C.GREEN}{host}:{port_s}{C.RESET}  '
                f'[{C.GRAY}client: {cid}{C.RESET}]'
            )
            if self.on_connection:
                self.on_connection('CONNECT', host, port_s, cid, ts)
            return

        # TCP Connect Fail
        m = self.LINE_PATTERNS['connect_fail'].search(body)
        if m:
            cid = m.group(1)
            reason = m.group(2).strip()
            self.log('ADB', 'FAIL', f'TCP connect failed: {reason}  [{cid}]')
            if self.on_connection:
                self.on_connection('FAIL', '', '', cid, ts, reason=reason)
            return

        # Transfer stats
        m = self.LINE_PATTERNS['transfer'].search(body)
        if m:
            cid, direction, kb = m.group(1), m.group(2), int(m.group(3))
            if cid in self._connections:
                if direction == 'C->T':
                    self._connections[cid]['up'] += kb
                else:
                    self._connections[cid]['down'] += kb
            return

        # Pipe closed (direction-specific timeout/error)
        m = self.LINE_PATTERNS['pipe_closed'].search(body)
        if m:
            cid = m.group(1)
            direction = m.group(2)
            reason = m.group(3)
            self.log(
                'ADB', 'PIPE',
                f'{C.YELLOW}{direction}{C.RESET} {C.RED}{reason}{C.RESET}  [{C.GRAY}{cid}{C.RESET}]'
            )
            return

        # Connection closed
        if m:
            cid = m.group(1)
            active = int(m.group(2))
            if cid in self._connections:
                conn = self._connections[cid]
                self.log(
                    'ADB', 'DISC',
                    f'{C.GRAY}{conn["target"]}:{conn["port"]}{C.RESET}  '
                    f'sent={conn["up"]}KB  recv={conn["down"]}KB  '
                    f'active={active}'
                )
                if self.on_connection:
                    self.on_connection(
                        'DISCONNECT', conn['target'], str(conn['port']),
                        cid, ts, up=conn['up'], down=conn['down']
                    )
                del self._connections[cid]
            return

        # UDP Associate
        m = self.LINE_PATTERNS['udp_associate'].search(body)
        if m:
            cid, port = m.group(1), m.group(2)
            self.log('ADB', 'UDP', f'UDP ASSOCIATE port {port}  [{cid}]')
            return

        # UDP Cleanup
        m = self.LINE_PATTERNS['udp_cleanup'].search(body)
        if m:
            key = m.group(1)
            self.log('ADB', 'UDP', f'{C.YELLOW}UDP relay cleaned up: {key}{C.RESET}')
            return

        # Server events
        if self.LINE_PATTERNS['server_start'].search(body):
            self.log('ADB', 'OK', f'{C.GREEN}Proxy server started on phone{C.RESET}')
            return

        if self.LINE_PATTERNS['server_stop'].search(body):
            self.log('ADB', 'STOP', f'{C.RED}Proxy server stopping{C.RESET}')
            return


class TunMonitor:
    """Monitors TUN adapter throughput via PowerShell."""

    def __init__(self, log_fn, interval=2.0):
        self.log = log_fn
        self.interval = interval
        self._running = False
        self._prev_rx = None
        self._prev_tx = None
        self._prev_time = None

    def start(self):
        self._running = True
        threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True).start()

    def stop(self):
        self._running = False

    def _get_stats(self):
        script = '''
        $adapter = Get-NetAdapter -Name "LaptopProxyVPN" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        if (-not $adapter) { return "NOT_FOUND" }
        $stats = Get-NetAdapterStatistics -Name "LaptopProxyVPN" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        if (-not $stats) { return "NOT_FOUND" }
        return "$($stats.ReceivedBytes),$($stats.SentBytes),$($adapter.Status)"
        '''
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(
                ['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', script],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10
            )
            out = res.stdout.strip()
            if out == "NOT_FOUND":
                return None
            parts = out.split(',')
            if len(parts) == 3:
                return {
                    'rx': int(parts[0]),
                    'tx': int(parts[1]),
                    'status': parts[2].strip()
                }
        except Exception:
            pass
        return None

    def _run(self):
        time.sleep(3)  # Wait for initial setup
        while self._running:
            stats = self._get_stats()
            now = time.time()

            if stats is None:
                self.log('TUN', 'WAIT', 'Adapter not ready yet...')
                time.sleep(self.interval)
                continue

            if self._prev_rx is not None and self._prev_tx is not None:
                dt = now - self._prev_time
                if dt > 0:
                    rx_bps = (stats['rx'] - self._prev_rx) / dt
                    tx_bps = (stats['tx'] - self._prev_tx) / dt

                    rx_str = self._format_bps(rx_bps)
                    tx_str = self._format_bps(tx_bps)

                    status_flag = ''
                    if rx_bps < 100 and tx_bps < 100:
                        status_flag = f' {C.YELLOW}(idle){C.RESET}'

                    self.log(
                        'TUN', 'STAT',
                        f'↓ {C.GREEN}{rx_str}/s{C.RESET}  ↑ {C.CYAN}{tx_str}/s{C.RESET}{status_flag}  '
                        f'[{C.GRAY}{stats["status"]}{C.RESET}]'
                    )

            self._prev_rx = stats['rx']
            self._prev_tx = stats['tx']
            self._prev_time = now

            time.sleep(self.interval)

    @staticmethod
    def _format_bps(bps):
        if bps >= 1048576:
            return f'{bps / 1048576:.1f}MB'
        elif bps >= 1024:
            return f'{bps / 1024:.1f}KB'
        else:
            return f'{bps:.0f}B'


class SysMonitor:
    """Monitors system-level events: process health, network changes."""

    def __init__(self, log_fn, interval=5.0):
        self.log = log_fn
        self.interval = interval
        self._running = False
        self._last_tun2socks_alive = True
        self._last_adb_alive = True

    def start(self):
        self._running = True
        threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True).start()

    def stop(self):
        self._running = False

    def _process_running(self, name):
        script = f'Get-Process -Name "{name}" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Id'
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(
                ['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', script],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
            )
            return bool(res.stdout.strip())
        except Exception:
            return False

    def _adb_devices(self):
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(
                ['adb', 'devices'],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
            )
            lines = res.stdout.strip().splitlines()
            devices = [l for l in lines if l and not l.startswith('List') and 'device' in l]
            return len(devices)
        except Exception:
            return 0

    def _run(self):
        time.sleep(5)
        while self._running:
            t2s_alive = self._process_running('tun2socks')
            adb_count = self._adb_devices()

            if t2s_alive and not self._last_tun2socks_alive:
                self.log('SYS', 'OK', 'tun2socks process started')
            elif not t2s_alive and self._last_tun2socks_alive:
                self.log('SYS', 'FAIL', f'{C.RED}tun2socks process CRASHED{C.RESET}')

            if adb_count == 0 and self._last_adb_alive:
                self.log('SYS', 'FAIL', f'{C.RED}ADB device disconnected{C.RESET}')
            elif adb_count > 0 and not self._last_adb_alive:
                self.log('SYS', 'OK', f'ADB device reconnected ({adb_count} device(s))')

            self._last_tun2socks_alive = t2s_alive
            self._last_adb_alive = adb_count > 0

            time.sleep(self.interval)


class CsvLogger:
    """Writes structured connection log to CSV for analysis."""

    def __init__(self, filepath):
        self.filepath = filepath
        self.fp = open(filepath, 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8')
        self.writer = csv.writer(self.fp)
        self.writer.writerow([
            'Timestamp', 'Event', 'Target_Host', 'Target_Port',
            'Client_ID', 'Sent_KB', 'Recv_KB', 'Active_Count', 'Detail'
        ])
        self.fp.flush()

    def write(self, ts, event, host='', port='', cid='', up=0, down=0, active='', detail=''):
        self.writer.writerow([
            ts, event, host, port, cid, up, down, active, detail
        ])
        self.fp.flush()

    def close(self):
        self.fp.close()


class TrafficMonitor:
    """Orchestrates all monitoring sources."""

    def __init__(self, log_dir=DEFAULT_LOG_DIR, use_adb=True):
        self.log_dir = log_dir
        self.use_adb = use_adb
        self._running = False
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._log_file = None

        os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # Open log files
        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
        self.log_path = os.path.join(log_dir, f'traffic_log_{timestamp}.txt')
        self.csv_path = os.path.join(log_dir, f'traffic_log_{timestamp}.csv')
        self._log_file = open(self.log_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8')
        self.csv_log = CsvLogger(self.csv_path)

        # Sub-monitors
        if use_adb:
            self.adb = AdbMonitor(
                log_fn=self._on_log,
                on_connection=self._on_conn_event
            )
        else:
            self.adb = None

        self.tun = TunMonitor(log_fn=self._on_log)
        self.sys = SysMonitor(log_fn=self._on_log)

    @staticmethod
    def _sanitize(text):
        # Replace Unicode chars that break Windows console (cp1252)
        return text.replace('\u2713', '+').replace('\u2717', 'x').replace('\u2014', '--')

    def _on_log(self, source, event, detail):
        ts = datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S')
        safe_detail = self._sanitize(detail)
        with self._lock:
            line = f'{C.GRAY}[{ts}]{C.RESET} {C.BOLD}{source:<5}{C.RESET} {event:<5} {safe_detail}'
            try:
                print(line, flush=True)
            except UnicodeEncodeError:
                print(line.encode('utf-8', errors='replace').decode('utf-8', errors='replace'), flush=True)
            plain = f'[{ts}] {source} {event} {self._sanitize(re.sub(r'\033\[[0-9;]*m', '', detail))}'
            self._log_file.write(plain + '\n')
            self._log_file.flush()

    def _on_conn_event(self, event, host, port, cid, ts, **kwargs):
        if event == 'CONNECT':
            self.csv_log.write(
                ts, 'CONNECT', host, port, cid,
                detail=f'TCP connected to {host}:{port}'
            )
        elif event == 'DISCONNECT':
            self.csv_log.write(
                ts, 'DISCONNECT', host, port, cid,
                up=kwargs.get('up', 0), down=kwargs.get('down', 0),
                detail=f'Closed. sent={kwargs.get("up", 0)}KB recv={kwargs.get("down", 0)}KB'
            )
        elif event == 'FAIL':
            self.csv_log.write(
                ts, 'FAIL', host, port, cid,
                detail=f'Connection failed: {kwargs.get("reason", "")}'
            )

    def start(self):
        self._running = True

        print(f'\n{C.BOLD}{C.CYAN}{"="*60}{C.RESET}')
        print(f'{C.BOLD}{C.CYAN}  Hotspot Bypass VPN — Traffic Monitor{C.RESET}')
        print(f'{C.BOLD}{C.CYAN}{"-"*60}{C.RESET}')
        print(f'  Log file:  {self.log_path}')
        print(f'  CSV file:   {self.csv_path}')
        print(f'  ADB:        {"Enabled" if self.use_adb else "Disabled"}')
        print(f'{C.BOLD}{C.CYAN}{"="*60}{C.RESET}\n')

        self._log_file.write(f'Traffic Monitor started — {datetime.now().isoformat()}\n')
        self._log_file.write(f'ADB: {"enabled" if self.use_adb else "disabled"}\n\n')

        if self.adb:
            self.adb.start()
        self.tun.start()
        self.sys.start()

    def stop(self):
        self._running = False
        if self.adb:
            self.adb.stop()
        self.tun.stop()
        self.sys.stop()

        if self._log_file:
            self._log_file.write(f'\nMonitor stopped — {datetime.now().isoformat()}\n')
            self._log_file.close()

        self.csv_log.close()

        print(f'\n{C.CYAN}Log saved: {self.log_path}{C.RESET}')
        print(f'{C.CYAN}CSV saved: {self.csv_path}{C.RESET}')

    def run(self):
        try:
            self.start()
            print(f'{C.GRAY}Press Ctrl+C to stop...{C.RESET}')
            while self._running:
                time.sleep(0.5)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print(f'\n{C.YELLOW}Stopping...{C.RESET}')
        finally:
            self.stop()


def check_adb():
    """Verify ADB is accessible and at least one device is connected."""
    try:
        res = subprocess.run(['adb', 'devices'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
        lines = res.stdout.strip().splitlines()
        devices = [l for l in lines if l and not l.startswith('List') and 'device' in l]
        unauthorized = [l for l in lines if l and 'unauthorized' in l]
        if unauthorized:
            print(f'{C.RED}Phone detected but unauthorized. Check the phone and allow USB debugging.{C.RESET}')
            return False
        if not devices:
            print(f'{C.YELLOW}No ADB devices found. Connect your phone via USB.{C.RESET}')
            print(f'{C.YELLOW}  or use --no-adb to run without phone logs.{C.RESET}')
            return False
        print(f'{C.GREEN}ADB OK — {len(devices)} device(s) connected{C.RESET}')
        return True
    except FileNotFoundError:
        print(f'{C.YELLOW}adb.exe not found in PATH. Use --no-adb to skip.{C.RESET}')
        return False
    except Exception as e:
        print(f'{C.YELLOW}ADB check failed: {e}{C.RESET}')
        return False


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description='Hotspot Bypass VPN — Real-time Traffic Monitor',
        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
        epilog=(
            'Examples:\n'
            '  python traffic_monitor.py\n'
            '  python traffic_monitor.py --no-adb\n'
            '  python traffic_monitor.py --log-dir ./my_logs\n'
        )
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        '--log-dir', default=DEFAULT_LOG_DIR,
        help=f'Directory for log files (default: {DEFAULT_LOG_DIR})'
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        '--no-adb', action='store_true',
        help='Skip ADB phone logs (TUN stats + system monitoring only)'
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    use_adb = not args.no_adb
    if use_adb:
        if not check_adb():
            print(f'{C.YELLOW}Continuing with ADB disabled.{C.RESET}')
            use_adb = False

    monitor = TrafficMonitor(log_dir=args.log_dir, use_adb=use_adb)
    monitor.run()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

import pathlib

PATH = pathlib.Path(r"C:\Users\user\AndroidStudioProjects\HotspotBypassVPN\HotspotBypassVPN\laptop_proxy\main.py")
content = PATH.read_text("utf-8")
lines = content.split("\n")

# Find boundaries
app_start = None
main_end = None
for i, line in enumerate(lines):
    if line.startswith("class App:") and app_start is None:
        app_start = i
    if app_start is not None and "root.mainloop()" in line:
        main_end = i
        break

print(f"Replacing lines {app_start+1} through {main_end+1}")

# Read new code from separate file
new_code_path = pathlib.Path(r"C:\Users\user\AndroidStudioProjects\HotspotBypassVPN\HotspotBypassVPN\laptop_proxy\_new_ui_code.py")
new_code = new_code_path.read_text("utf-8")

before = "\n".join(lines[:app_start])
after = "\n".join(lines[main_end+1:])

new_content = before + "\n" + new_code + "\n" + after
PATH.write_text(new_content, "utf-8")
print("Done!")
